[h2]getFont public [Font](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/font-0000001054238751) getFont​() Obtains the text font. **Returns:** Returns the text font. **See Also:** [setFont(Font)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setFont-ohos_agp_text_Font-) [h2]setTruncationMode public void setTruncationMode​([Text.TruncationMode](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_truncationmode-0000001054678693) mode) Sets the truncation mode when text content is longer than the component's width. When a text component cannot show all of the text content in a single line, the following modes can be selected: (1) the start, middle, or end of the text is truncated and the truncated part is displayed as an ellipsis, (2) the text will be scrolling to display the complete content or (3) truncate directly without any special processing. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
mode  |  Indicates the truncation mode.

---|---  
resId  |  Indicates the [Element](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/element-0000001054358705) resource id of the cursor in the text.

**Since:** 7 [h2]getCursorElement public [Element](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/element-0000001054358705) getCursorElement​() Obtains the element object of the cursor in a text. **Returns:** Returns the element object of the cursor in the text. **See Also:** [setCursorElement(Element)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setCursorElement-ohos_agp_components_element_Element-) [h2]setAroundElements public void setAroundElements​([Element](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/element-0000001054358705) left, [Element](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/element-0000001054358705) top, [Element](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/element-0000001054358705) right, [Element](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/element-0000001054358705) bottom) Sets element objects around a text. This method places element objects to the left, on the top, to the right, and at the bottom of a text. The sides of the element objects are determined by how the text is read.  Note: if element's bounds didn't set then this method set it by optimal sizes. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
left  |  Indicates the element object to the left of the text.  
top  |  Indicates the element object on the top the text.  
right  |  Indicates the element object to the right of the text.  
bottom  |  Indicates the element object at the bottom of the text.

If pos is AUTO_CURSOR_POSITION, the text is inserted in the position where the cursor is located. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
text  |  Indicates the text to insert.  
pos  |  Indicates the position where to insert the text. The value AUTO_CURSOR_POSITION indicates to insert the text from the position where the cursor is located.

---|---  
length  |  Indicates the length of the text to delete.

---|---  
length  |  Indicates the length of the text to delete.  
back  |  Indicates whether to delete the text in the backward manner. true indicates to delete the text before the cursor; false indicates to delete the text after the cursor.  
**Since:** 2 [h2]delete public void delete​(int length, boolean back, int pos) Deletes text from a specified position. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
length  |  Indicates the length of the text to delete.  
back  |  Indicates whether to delete the text in the backward manner. true indicates to delete the text before the specified position; false indicates to delete the text after the specified position.  
pos  |  Indicates the position where to delete the text. The value AUTO_CURSOR_POSITION indicates to delete the text from the position where the cursor is located.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws the exception if the parameter value is less than 0.

---|---  
autoFontSize  |  Indicates whether the text size is automatically adjusted to fill within the component. true indicates the text size is automatically adjusted to fill within the component; false indicates the text size is not automatically adjusted to fill within the component.  
**See Also:** [isAutoFontSize()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__isAutoFontSize--) [h2]setAutoFontSize public void setAutoFontSize​(boolean autoFontSize, boolean userSizeFirst) Sets whether to adjust font size automatically to fill within the component, and whether to try the specified font size before enabling automatic font size adaption. It is recommended that you enable autoFontSize before enabling userSizeFirst. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
autoFontSize  |  Indicates whether to enable automatic font size adaption. true indicates to adjust font size automatically to fill within the component, so that the complete text content can be displayed; false indicates the opposite.  
userSizeFirst  |  userSizeFirst Indicates whether to try the specified font size before enabling automatic font size adaption. true indicates that the specified font size is tried for the layout. If the specified font size does not allow all the text content to be displayed in the component, a suitable font size set by [setAutoFontSizeRule(int,int,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setAutoFontSizeRule-int-int-int-) will be selected. false indicates that a suitable font size set by [setAutoFontSizeRule(int,int ,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setAutoFontSizeRule-int-int-int-) will be used for the layout directly, without trying the specified one.  
**Since:** 3 **See Also:** [setAutoFontSize(boolean)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setAutoFontSize-boolean-) [h2]setLineSpacing public void setLineSpacing​(float add, float mult) Sets the line spacing for a text component. Each line has its height multiplied by mult, with add added to it. For example, when the height is 1, mult is 2, and add is 3, the line spacing is 5. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
add  |  Indicates the value to be added to the height of each line except for the last line.  
mult  |  Indicates the value to be multiplied by the height of each line, except for the last line.

**Since:** 4 [h2]getPaddingForText public boolean getPaddingForText​() Checks whether the text has extra padding spaces. **Returns:** Returns true if the height of the text is between the top and bottom borders; returns false if the height is between the font ascent and descent. **Since:** 4 [h2]getFontVariations public [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) getFontVariations​() Obtains the font variation settings. **Returns:** Returns the current font variation settings if obtained; returns an empty string if none has been specified. **Since:** 5 **See Also:** [setFontVariations(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setFontVariations-java_lang_String-) [h2]setFontVariations public boolean setFontVariations​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) variation) Sets the TrueType or OpenType font variation settings. The settings string consists of multiple pairs of tag and style values. The tag must contain exact four ASCII characters and must be wrapped in single quotes or double quotes. If it contains characters outside of U+0020..U+007E, it is invalid. If a specified tag name is not yet defined in the font, the settings will be ignored.  The following shows sample code for setting the font width and weight to 100. 
    
    text.setFontVariations("'wdth' 100,'wght' 100");
     
     

**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
variation  |  Indicates the font variation settings.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the passed variation is valid for the current font; returns false if the passed variation is an empty string or does not comply with the defined specifications. **Since:** 5 **See Also:** [getFontVariations()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__getFontVariations--) [h2]getLineCount public int getLineCount​() Obtains the number of lines in this text. **Returns:** Returns the number of lines. **Since:** 7 [h2]getLineHeight public int getLineHeight​() Obtains the height between lines for this [Text](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676). **Returns:** Returns the height between lines. **Since:** 7 [h2]getCombinedPaddingStart public int getCombinedPaddingStart​() Obtains the combined padding at the beginning of this text. The conversion is used only for the text display, and will not change the text content. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
conversionType  |  Indicates the conversion type.  
**Since:** 7 **See Also:** [getTextConversion()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__getTextConversion--) [h2]getTextConversion public [Text.ConversionType](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975) getTextConversion​() Obtains the [ConversionType](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975) of this [Text](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676). The default conversion type is [ConversionType#NONE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001218157975__NONE). **Returns:** Returns the [ConversionType](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975). **Since:** 7 **See Also:** [setTextConversion(ohos.agp.components.Text.ConversionType)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setTextConversion-ohos_agp_components_Text_ConversionType-) [h2]isAllUpperCase public boolean isAllUpperCase​() Checks whether the [ConversionType](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975) applied to this [Text](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676) is set to [ConversionType#ALL_UPPERCASE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001218157975__ALL_UPPERCASE). **Returns:** Returns true if the [ConversionType](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975) is set to [ConversionType#ALL_UPPERCASE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text_conversiontype-0000001218157975#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001218157975__ALL_UPPERCASE); returns false otherwise. **Since:** 7 **See Also:** [setTextConversion(ConversionType)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setTextConversion-ohos_agp_components_Text_ConversionType-) [h2]isInputMethodActive public boolean isInputMethodActive​() Checks whether this [Text](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676) is the target of the current input method. **Returns:** Returns true if the text is the target of the current input method; false otherwise. **Since:** 7 [h2]onTextEditorAction public boolean onTextEditorAction​(int actionCode) Called when change events of the specified editor action are processed. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
actionCode  |  Indicates the editor action. For example, [InputAttribute#ENTER_KEY_TYPE_GO](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/inputattribute-0000001054440003#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440003__ENTER_KEY_TYPE_GO) indicates a "go" action, and [InputAttribute#ENTER_KEY_TYPE_SEND](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/inputattribute-0000001054440003#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440003__ENTER_KEY_TYPE_SEND) indicates a "send" action. For details, see [InputAttribute](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/inputattribute-0000001054440003).  
**Returns:** Returns true if the event is processed; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 7 [h2]isSoftInputVisibleOnFocus public boolean isSoftInputVisibleOnFocus​() Checks whether the soft input will be made visible when this text gains the focus. The default value is true. **Returns:** Returns true if the soft input will be made visible; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 7 [h2]setSoftInputVisibleOnFocus public void setSoftInputVisibleOnFocus​(boolean isShow) Sets whether the soft input will be made visible when this text gains the focus. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
isShow  |  Specifies whether to make the soft input visible. The value true means to make the soft input visible, and false means the opposite. The default value is true.  
**Since:** 7 [h2]getEditingText public boolean getEditingText​([EditingCapability](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/editingcapability-0000001060411187) request, [EditingText](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/editingtext-0000001061156874) text) Obtains the text that can be edited. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
request  |  Indicates a request from the input method.  
text  |  Indicates the text to check.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the editable text is obtained; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 7 [h2]setEditable public void setEditable​(boolean isEditable) Sets the editability for this text. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
isEditable  |  Specifies whether to set the text to be editable. The value true means to set the text to be editable, and false means the opposite.  
**Since:** 7 **See Also:** [isEditable()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__isEditable--) [h2]getExtraInputData public [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) getExtraInputData​(boolean isCreate) Obtains extra input data sent to the input method. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
isCreate  |  Specifies whether to create data if the data does not exist. The value true means to create the data, and false means the opposite.  
**Returns:** Returns the [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) object provided to the input method if obtained; returns null otherwise. **Since:** 7 **See Also:** [setExtraInputData(ohos.utils.PacMap)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/text-0000001054838676#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054838676__setExtraInputData-ohos_utils_PacMap-) [h2]onCustomInputMethodCommand public boolean onCustomInputMethodCommand​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) action, [PacMap](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/pacmap-0000001054440291) data) Called by the framework to respond to a custom command from the input method. By default, this method does nothing. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
action  |  Indicates the name of the command. .

The default value is true. **Returns:** Returns true if ascent or descent metrics are enabled for fallback fonts; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 7 [h2]setFallbackFontMetricsEnabled public void setFallbackFontMetricsEnabled​(boolean isEnable) Sets whether to enable ascent or descent metrics for fallback fonts. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
isEnable  |  Specifies whether to enable ascent or descent metrics for fallback fonts. The value true means to enable ascent or descent metrics for fallback fonts, and false means the opposite. The default value is true.
